   scaler = MinMaxScaler() if method == 'minmax' else StandardScaler()\n            schema[key] = scaler\n            return scaler.fit_transform(data)\n        return schema[key].transform(data)\n\n    def _process_data(self, target=\"fit\"):\n        \"\"\"\n        read and return data as x and y\n        @return: list of separate x and y\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self.model_type != len(self.target) > 0\n            ), \"please provide at least a target to predict\"\n\n        try:\n            read_data_options = self.dataset_props.get(\"read_data_options\", {})\n list(dataset.columns)\n            logger.info(f\"dataset attributes: {attributes}\")\n\n            fitting = self.command == \"fit\"\n            targets = self.target if self.model_type != \"clustering\" else []\n            if fitting:\n                self.feature_schema = fit_feature_schema(dataset, targets, self.dataset_props.get(\"features\"))\n            schema = getattr(self, \"feature_schema\", None)\n            if schema:\n                inputs = apply_feature_schema(dataset, schema)\n                if target not in (\"predict\", \"fit_cluster\"):\n                    missing_targets = [name for name in targets if name not in dataset]\n                    if missing_targets:\n                        raise ValueError(f\"Missing target columns: {missing_targets}\")\n                    dataset = pd.concat([inputs, dataset[targets]], axis=1)\n                else:\n                    dataset = inputs\n                attributes = list(dataset.columns)\n\n            # handle missing values in the dataset\n            preprocess_props = self.dataset_props.get(\"preprocess\", None)\n            if preprocess_props:\n                # handle encoding\n                encoding = preprocess_props.get(\"encoding\")\n                if encoding:\n                    encoding_type = encoding.get(\"type\", None)\n                    column = encoding.get(\"column\", None)\n                    if column in attributes:\n                        if schema and not fitting and encoding_type.lower() == 'labelencoding':\n                            classes_map = schema.get('label_encoding_classes', {})\n                            mapped = dataset[column].map(classes_map)\n                            if (mapped.isna() & dataset[column].notna()).any():\n                                raise ValueError(f'Unknown label in column: {column}')\n                            dataset[column] = mapped\n                        else:\n                            dataset, classes_map = encode(\n                                df=dataset,\n                                encoding_type=encoding_type.lower(),\n                                column=column,\n                            )\n                            if schema and classes_map:\n                                schema['label_encoding_classes'] = classes_map\n                        if classes_map:\n                            self.dataset_props[\n                                \"label_encoding_classes\"\n                            ] = classes_map\n                            logger.info(\n                                f\"adding classes_map to dataset props: \\n{classes_map}\"\n                            )\n                        logger.info(\n                            f\"shape of the dataset after encoding => {dataset.shape}\"\n                        )\n\n                # preprocessing strategy: mean, median, mode etc..\n                strategy = preprocess_props.get(\"missing_values\")\n                if strategy:\n                    if schema and strategy.lower() != 'drop':\n                        imputers = schema.setdefault('imputers', {})\n                        for col in dataset.columns:\n                            if fitting:\n                                imputer = SimpleImputer(strategy=strategy)\n                                values = imputer.fit_transform(dataset[[col]])\n                                if values.shape[1] != 1:\n                                    raise ValueError(f'Cannot impute empty column: {col}')\n                                imputers[col] = imputer\n                            elif col in imputers:\n                                values = imputers[col].transform(dataset[[col]])\n                            else:\n                                continue\n                            dataset[col] = values[:, 0]\n                    else:\n                        dataset = handle_missing_values(dataset, strategy=strategy)\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"shape of the dataset after handling missing values => {dataset.shape}\"\n                    )\n\n            if schema:\n                feature_columns = [col for col in dataset.columns if col not in targets]\n                if fitting:\n                    schema['processed_features'] = feature_columns\n                else:\n                    feature_data = dataset.reindex(columns=schema['processed_features'], fill_value=0)\n                    if target not in (\"predict\", \"fit_cluster\"):\n                        dataset = pd.concat([feature_data, dataset[targets]], axis=1)\n                    else:\n                        dataset = feature_data\n\n            if                   return x\n                else:\n                    scaling_method = scaling_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                    return self._scale_data(x, scaling_method,               )\n\n            y = pd.concat([dataset.pop(x) for x in self.target], axis=1)\n            x = _reshape(dataset.to_numpy())\n            y = _reshape(y.to_numpy())\n            logger.info(f\"y shape: {y.shape} and x shape: {x.shape}\")\n\n            # handle data scaling\n            if preprocess_props:\n                scaling_props = preprocess_props.get(\"scale\", None)\n                if scaling_props:\n                    scaling_method = scaling_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                    scaling_target = scaling_props.get(\"target\", None)\n                    if scaling_target == \"all\":\n                        x = self._scale_data(x, scaling_method, 'input_scaler')\n                        y = self._scale_data(y, scaling_method, 'target_scaler')\n                    elif scaling_target == \"inputs\":\n                        x = self._scale_data(x, scaling_method, 'input_scaler')\n                    elif scaling_target == \"outputs\":\n                        y = self._scale_data(y, scaling_method, 'target_scaler')\n\n            if target == \"evaluate\":\n                return x, y\n\n            split_options = self.dataset_props.get(\"split\", None)\n            if not split_options:\n                return x, y, None, None\n            test_size = split_options.get(\"test_size\")\n            shuffle =        )\n\n            return x_train, y_train, x_test, y_test\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"error occured while preparing the data: {e}\")\n            raise\n\n    def _prepare_clustering_data(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        preprocess data for the clustering algorithm\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._process_data(target=\"fit_cluster\")\n\n    def model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=False,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(e)\n            res = evaluate_model(\n                model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=True,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        return res\n\n    def fit(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        fit a machine learning model and save it to a file along with a description.json file\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        x_train = None\n        x_test = None\n        y_train = None\n        y_test = None\n        cv_results = None\n        eval_results = None\n        cv_params = None\n        hp_search_results = {}\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            x_train = self._prepare_clustering_data()\n        else:\n            x_train, y_train, x_test, y_test = self._prepare_fit_data()\n        self.model, model_args = self._create_model(**kwargs)\n        logger.info(f\"executing a {self.model.__class__.__name__} algorithm...\")\n\n        # convert to multioutput if there is more than perform cross validation\n                logger.info(\"performing cross validation ...\")\n                cv_results = cross_validate(\n                    estimator=self.model, X=x_train, y=y_train, **cv_params\n                )\n            hyperparams_props = self.model_props.get(\n                \"hyperparameter_search\", None\n            )\n            if hyperparams_props:\n\n                # if the model type is clustering\n            self.model.fit(x_train)\n\n        saved = self._save_model(self.model)\n        if saved:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"model saved successfully and can be found in the {self.results_path} folder\"\n            )\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            eval_results = self.model.score(x_train)\n        else:\n            if x_test is None:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"no split options was provided. training score will be calculated\"\n                )\n                eval_results = self.model.score(x_train, y_train)\n\n            else:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"split option detected. The performance will be automatically evaluated \"\n                    f\"using the test data portion\"\n                )\n                y_pred = self.model.predict(x_test)\n                eval_results = self.get_evaluation(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    x_test=x_test,\n                    y_true=y_test,\n                    y_pred=y_pred,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n\n        schema_path = Path(self.results_path) / 'feature_schema.joblib'\n        joblib.dump(self.feature_schema, schema_path)\n        fit_description = {\n json.dump(fit_description, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(\n                f\"Error while storing the fit description file: {e}\"\n            )\n\n    def evaluate(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        evaluate a pre-fitted model and save results to a evaluation.json\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        x_val = None\n        y_true = None\n        eval_results = None\n\n        try:\n            model = self._load_model(f=self.model_path)\n            if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n                x_val,    scaler = MinMaxScaler() if method == 'minmax' else StandardScaler()\n            schema[key] = scaler\n            return scaler.fit_transform(data)\n        return schema[key].transform(data)\n\n    def _process_data(self, target=\"fit\"):\n        \"\"\"\n        read and return data as x and y\n        @return: list of separate x and y\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self.model_type != len(self.target) > 0\n            ), \"please provide at least a target to predict\"\n\n        try:\n            read_data_options = self.dataset_props.get(\"read_data_options\", {})\n \"clustering\" else []\n            if fitting:\n                self.feature_schema = fit_feature_schema(dataset, targets, self.dataset_props.get(\"features\"))\n            schema = getattr(self,                    dataset = pd.concat([inputs, dataset[targets]], axis=1)\n                else:\n                    dataset = inputs\n                attributes = list(dataset.columns)\n\n            # handle missing values in the dataset\n            preprocess_props = self.dataset_props.get(\"preprocess\", None)\n            if preprocess_props:\n                # handle encoding\n                encoding = preprocess_props.get(\"encoding\")\n                if encoding:\n                    encoding_type = encoding.get(\"type\", None)\n                    column = encoding.get(\"column\", None)\n                    if column in attributes:\n                        if schema and not fitting and encoding_type.lower() == 'labelencoding':\n                            classes_map = schema.get('label_encoding_classes', {})\n                            mapped = dataset[column].map(classes_map)\n                            if (mapped.isna() & dataset[column].notna()).any():\n                                raise ValueError(f'Unknown label in column: {column}')\n                            dataset[column] = mapped\n                        else:\n                            dataset, classes_map = encode(\n                                df=dataset,\n                                encoding_type=encoding_type.lower(),\n                                column=column,\n                            )\n                            if schema and classes_map:\n                                schema['label_encoding_classes'] = classes_map\n                        if logger.info(\n                                f\"adding classes_map to dataset props: \\n{classes_map}\"\n                            )\n                        logger.info(\n                            f\"shape of the dataset after encoding => {dataset.shape}\"\n                        )\n\n                # preprocessing strategy: mean, median, mode etc..\n                strategy = preprocess_props.get(\"missing_values\")\n                if strategy:\n                    if schema and strategy.lower() != 'drop':\n                        imputers = schema.setdefault('imputers', {})\n                        for col in dataset.columns:\n                            if fitting:\n                                imputer = SimpleImputer(strategy=strategy)\n                                values = imputer.fit_transform(dataset[[col]])\n                                if values.shape[1] != 1:\n                                    raise ValueError(f'Cannot impute empty column: {col}')\n                                imputers[col] = imputer\n                            elif col in imputers:\n                                values = imputers[col].transform(dataset[[col]])\n                            else:\n                                continue\n                            dataset[col] = values[:, 0]\n                    else:\n                        dataset = handle_missing_values(dataset, strategy=strategy)\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"shape of the dataset after handling missing values => {dataset.shape}\"\n                    )\n\n            if schema:\n                feature_columns = [col for col in dataset.columns if col not in targets]\n                if fitting:\n                    schema['processed_features'] \"fit_cluster\"):\n                        dataset = pd.concat([feature_data, dataset[targets]], axis=1)\n                    else:\n                        dataset = feature_data\n\n            if                   return x\n                else:\n                    scaling_method = scaling_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                    return self._scale_data(x, scaling_method,               )\n\n            y = pd.concat([dataset.pop(x) for x in self.target], axis=1)\n            x = _reshape(dataset.to_numpy())\n            y = _reshape(y.to_numpy())\n            logger.info(f\"y shape: {y.shape} and x shape: {x.shape}\")\n\n            # handle data scaling\n            if preprocess_props:\n                scaling_props = preprocess_props.get(\"scale\",          if scaling_target == \"all\":\n                        x = self._scale_data(x, scaling_method, 'input_scaler')\n                        y = self._scale_data(y, scaling_method, \"outputs\":\n                        y = self._scale_data(y, scaling_method, 'target_scaler')\n\n            if target == \"evaluate\":\n                return x, y\n\n            split_options =    def _prepare_clustering_data(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        preprocess data for the clustering algorithm\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._process_data(target=\"fit_cluster\")\n\n    def _prepare_predict_data(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        preprocess predict data to get similar data to the one used when training the model\n        \"\"\"\n        return model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=False,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(e)\n            res = evaluate_model(\n                model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=True,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        return res\n\n    def fit(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        fit a machine learning model and save it to a file along with a description.json file\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        x_train = None\n        x_test = None\n        y_train = None\n        y_test = None\n        cv_results = None\n        eval_results = None\n        cv_params = None\n        hp_search_results = {}\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            x_train = self._prepare_clustering_data()\n        else:\n            x_train, y_train, x_test, y_test = self._prepare_fit_data()\n        self.model, model_args = self._create_model(**kwargs)\n        logger.info(f\"executing a {self.model.__class__.__name__} algorithm...\")\n\n        # convert to multioutput if there is more than one target to predict:\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\" and len(self.target) > 1:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"predicting multiple targets detected. Hence, the model will be automatically \"\n                f\"converted to a multioutput model\"\n            )\n            self.model = (\n                MultiOutputClassifier(self.model)\n                if self.model_type == \"classification\"\n                else MultiOutputRegressor(self.model)\n            )\n\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n            cv_params = self.model_props.get(\"cross_validate\", None)\n            if not cv_params:\n                logger.info(f\"cross validation is not provided\")\n            else:\n                # perform cross validation\n                logger.info(\"performing cross validation ...\")\n                cv_results = cross_validate(\n                    estimator=self.model, X=x_train, y=y_train, **cv_params\n                )\n            hyperparams_props = self.model_props.get(\n                \"hyperparameter_search\", None\n            )\n            if hyperparams_props:\n\n                # perform hyperparameter search\n                method = hyperparams_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                grid_params = hyperparams_props.get(\"parameter_grid\", None)\n                hp_args = hyperparams_props.get(\"arguments\", None)\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"Performing hyperparameter search using -> {method}\"\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"Grid parameters entered by the user: {grid_params}\"\n                )\n                logger.info(f\"Additional hyperparameter arguments: {hp_args}\")\n                best_estimator, best_params, best_score = hyperparameter_search(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    method=method,\n                    params=grid_params,\n                    x_train=x_train,\n                    y_train=y_train,\n                    **hp_args,\n                )\n                hp_search_results[\"best_params\"] = best_params\n                hp_search_results[\"best_score\"] = best_score\n                self.model = best_estimator\n\n            self.model.fit(x_train, y_train)\n\n        else:  # if the model type is clustering\n            self.model.fit(x_train)\n\n        saved = self._save_model(self.model)\n        if saved:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"model saved successfully and can be found in the {self.results_path} folder\"\n            )\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            eval_results = self.model.score(x_train)\n        else:\n            if x_test is None:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"no split options was provided. training score will be calculated\"\n                )\n                eval_results = self.model.score(x_train, y_train)\n\n            else:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"split option detected. The performance will be automatically evaluated \"\n                    f\"using the test data portion\"\n                )\n                y_pred = self.model.predict(x_test)\n                eval_results = self.get_evaluation(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    x_test=x_test,\n                    y_true=y_test,\n                    y_pred=y_pred,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n\n        schema_path = Path(self.results_path) / Constants.feature_schema_file\n        joblib.dump(self.feature_schema, schema_path)\n        fit_description = {\n logger.exception(\n                f\"Error while storing the fit description file: {e}\"\n            )\n\n    def evaluate(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        evaluate a pre-fitted model and       **kwargs,\n                )\n            else:\n                x_val = self._prepare_clustering_data()\n                y_pred = model.predict(x_val)\n                eval_results = _get_predictions(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        use a pre-fitted model to generate predictions\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            model =                self.target[i]: y_pred[:, i]\n                    if len(y_pred.shape) > 1\n                    else y_pred\n                    for i in range(len(self.target))\n                }\n            )\n            return df_pred\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"Error while preparing predictions: {e}\")\n            raise\n\n    def predict(self):\n
